page</button></div><div class="theme-doc-markdown markdown">\<header\>\<h1\>Chat rooms and private DMs</h1></header><div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>You need a module and a client connected to it. If you don&#x27;t have them yet, follow the <a class="" href="/docs/">quickstart</a> for your language first. This guide uses a minimal <code>player</code> table with a name, and doesn&#x27;t cover how players get their row. To let players pick a unique name and block offensive ones, see <a class="" href="/docs/how-to/social/unique-usernames">Keep usernames unique and block offensive ones</a>: the code here works the same with its <code>player</code> table.</p><p>For a single chat that everyone can read, see the <a class="" href="/docs/tutorials/chat-app">chat app tutorial</a> instead.</p></div></div>
<p>In this guide, you&#x27;ll let players create and join chat rooms, and send each other direct messages (DMs). Each player only receives the messages of the rooms they&#x27;re in, and their own DMs. You&#x27;ll store messages in private tables, and use <a class="" href="/docs/functions/views">views</a> built with the query builder to give each player their own messages.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="how-it-works">How it works<a href="#how-it-works" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to How it works" title="Direct link to How it works" translate="no">​</a></h2>
\<ul\>
<li class="">A public <code>player</code> table holds each player&#x27;s name. How a player gets their row is up to the rest of your module.</li>
<li class="">A public <code>chat_room</code> table lists the rooms, so that players can find rooms to join.</li>
<li class="">A private <code>room_member</code> table has one row for each player in each room.</li>
<li class="">A private <code>message</code> table holds the messages of every room, and a private <code>direct_message</code> table holds every DM.</li>
<li class="">Reducers create, join and leave rooms, and send messages to a room or to a player.</li>
<li class="">Views return the rooms the caller is in, the messages of those rooms, and the DMs the caller sent and received.</li>
<li class="">Clients subscribe to the room list and to the views, and sort the messages themselves.</li>
</ul>
<p>The <code>room_member</code>, <code>message</code> and <code>direct_message</code> tables are private, so clients can&#x27;t read who is in which room, or anyone else&#x27;s messages. They only see the rows the views return, and each view only returns the caller&#x27;s own rows.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="define-the-tables">Define the tables<a href="#define-the-tables" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Define the tables" title="Direct link to Define the tables" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add the five tables. The four new ones each have an <code>id</code> primary key that&#x27;s assigned automatically, in the order rows are created. <code>room_member</code> has an index on <code>room_id</code> and one on <code>member</code>, <code>message</code> has an index on <code>room_id</code>, and <code>direct_message</code> has an index on <code>sender</code> and one on <code>recipient</code>. These indexes let the reducers and views find each player&#x27;s rows without reading the whole table.</p>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { schema, table, t, SenderError, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> InferSchema, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ReducerCtx } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb/server</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> player </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BTree</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Message</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Id;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKeyAutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_member, id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_member, room_id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_member, member)</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Message</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> id;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Identity sender;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    Identity recipient;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_STRUCT</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(DirectMessage, id, sender, recipient, text, sent)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_TABLE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(DirectMessage, direct_message, Private)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_PrimaryKeyAutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(direct_message, id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(direct_message, sender)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">FIELD_Index</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(direct_message, recipient)</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="create-join-and-leave-rooms">Create, join and leave rooms<a href="#create-join-and-leave-rooms" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Create, join and leave rooms" title="Direct link to Create, join and leave rooms" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add a <code>require_player</code> function that rejects players who don&#x27;t have a <code>player</code> row yet, and a <code>find_membership</code> function that returns the row recording that a player is in a room, if there is one. <code>find_membership</code> reads the player&#x27;s rows through the <code>member</code> index, and looks for the room among them. The C++ module also needs a <code>trim</code> function, to remove the spaces at both ends of a string.</p>
<p>Then add three reducers:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>create_room</code> creates a room, and adds the caller to it. It rejects players who don&#x27;t have a <code>player</code> row yet, and room names that are empty or longer than 50 characters, after removing the spaces at both ends.</li>
<li class=""><code>join_room</code> adds the caller to a room. It rejects players who don&#x27;t have a <code>player</code> row yet, and rooms that don&#x27;t exist. Joining a room twice changes nothing, so a client that sends the request twice doesn&#x27;t get an error.</li>
<li class=""><code>leave_room</code> removes the caller from a room.</li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 50</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Throws unless the caller has a `player` row.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> requirePlayer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            ctx.Db.RoomMember.Id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row.Id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// Returns an error unless the caller has a `player` row.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Pick a name first"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// Returns the row that records `member` being in the room, if there is one.</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(member)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">room_id </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room_id)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> create_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><(), </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">trim</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">chars</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">count</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() > </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Room names must be 1 to {MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH} characters"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">chat_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ChatRoom</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">room_member</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">RoomMember</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        member</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> join_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><(), </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">chat_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_none</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Room not found"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, room_id, ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(()); </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// Already in the room.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">room_member</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">RoomMember</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        room_id,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        member</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> leave_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, room_id, ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">room_member</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Lengths are counted in bytes, so names and messages that aren&#x27;t plain ASCII hold fewer characters.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">constexpr</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> size_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 50</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Removes the spaces at both ends of `text`.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> trim</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> start </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find_first_not_of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> \t\r\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (start </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">npos) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> end </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find_last_not_of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> \t\r\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">substr</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(start, end </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> start </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Rejects players who don&#x27;t have a `player` row yet.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Outcome</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">void</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.db[player_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Pick a name first</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Returns the row that records `member` being in the room, if there is one.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">optional</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">RoomMember</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> room_id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Identity</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> member</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row : ctx.db[room_member_member].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(member)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (row.room_id </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room_id) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">nullopt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(create_room, ReducerContext ctx, std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string name) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (allowed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> trim</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (trimmed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">size</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Room names must be 1 to </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> +</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(MAX_ROOM_NAME_LENGTH) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> characters</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.db[chat_room].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ChatRoom{</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, trimmed});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db[room_member].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(RoomMember{</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room.id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(join_room, ReducerContext ctx, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room_id) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (allowed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.db[chat_room_id].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Room not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, room_id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span><span style="color:#6272A4"> // Already in the room.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db[room_member].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(RoomMember{</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(leave_room, ReducerContext ctx, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room_id) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, room_id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx.db[room_member_id].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete_by_key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="send-messages">Send messages<a href="#send-messages" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Send messages" title="Direct link to Send messages" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add a <code>message_text</code> function that removes the spaces at both ends of a message, and rejects messages that are then empty or longer than 1,000 characters. Then add two reducers:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>send_message</code> sends a message to a room. It rejects players who aren&#x27;t in the room.</li>
<li class=""><code>send_direct_message</code> sends a message to another player. It rejects players who don&#x27;t have a <code>player</code> row yet, messages to yourself, and messages to a player who doesn&#x27;t exist.</li>
</ul>
<p>Both reducers set the sender to <code>ctx.sender</code> and the time to <code>ctx.timestamp</code>, so clients can&#x27;t send messages in another player&#x27;s name, or change when a message was sent.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Removes the spaces at both ends of a message, and checks its length.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> messageText</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> text.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">trim</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (trimmed.length </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed.length </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Messages must be 1 to </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> characters`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> sendMessage </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { roomId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">roomId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">findMembership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, roomId, ctx.sender)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">You&#x27;re not in this room</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db.message.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      roomId,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.sender,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> messageText</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> sendDirectMessage </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { recipient</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">recipient</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    requirePlayer</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (recipient.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">isEqual</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">You can&#x27;t message yourself</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.db.player.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(recipient)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      throw</span><span style="color:#FF79C6;font-weight:bold"> new</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> SenderError</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Player not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db.directMessage.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      sender</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.sender,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      recipient,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> messageText</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      sent</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">constexpr</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> size_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Removes the spaces at both ends of a message, and checks its length.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Outcome</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">message_text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> trim</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (trimmed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> trimmed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">size</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Messages must be 1 to </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> +</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(MAX_MESSAGE_LENGTH) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> characters</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(trimmed);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(send_message, ReducerContext ctx, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">uint64_t</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room_id, std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string text) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find_membership</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, room_id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">You&#x27;re not in this room</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> clean </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> message_text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db[message].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Message{</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), ctx.timestamp});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_REDUCER</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(send_direct_message, ReducerContext ctx, Identity recipient, std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string text) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> require_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (allowed.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> allowed;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (recipient </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">You can&#x27;t message yourself</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.db[player_identity].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(recipient)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Player not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> clean </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> message_text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(text);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.db[direct_message].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(DirectMessage{</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), recipient, clean.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">value</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), ctx.timestamp});</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="show-each-player-their-messages">Show each player their messages<a href="#show-each-player-their-messages" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Show each player their messages" title="Direct link to Show each player their messages" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add four views:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>my_rooms</code> returns the rooms the caller is in.</li>
<li class=""><code>my_messages</code> returns the messages of the rooms the caller is in.</li>
<li class=""><code>my_sent_dms</code> returns the DMs the caller sent, and <code>my_received_dms</code> the DMs the caller received. A single view that combines both filters with <code>or</code> would also work, but SpacetimeDB can&#x27;t use an index for an <code>or</code>, so it would read every DM in the database each time a player subscribes. Two views each read only the caller&#x27;s DMs, through their own index.</li>
</ul>
<p><code>my_rooms</code> and <code>my_messages</code> start from the caller&#x27;s rows in <code>room_member</code>, then use a semijoin to keep the rooms, or the messages, that match them.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C++</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The rooms the caller is in.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> myRooms </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_rooms</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(chatRoom.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">  ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.from.roomMember</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">m</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.member.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">rightSemijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from.chatRoom, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">m</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.roomId.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room.id))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The messages of the rooms the caller is in.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> myMessages </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_messages</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(message.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">  ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.from.roomMember</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">m</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.member.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">rightSemijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from.message, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">m</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">msg</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.roomId.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(msg.roomId))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The DMs the caller sent.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> mySentDms </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_sent_dms</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(directMessage.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">  ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.directMessage.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">dm</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> dm.sender.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The DMs the caller received.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> myReceivedDms </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">view</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my_received_dms</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">array</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(directMessage.rowType),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">  ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from.directMessage.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">dm</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> dm.recipient.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // The rooms the caller is in.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">View</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">MyRooms</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IQuery</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ChatRoom</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">MyRooms</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ViewContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> me </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Sender;</span></span>
