<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> me </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[room_member]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">me</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> m</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) { </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.member.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(me); })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">right_semijoin</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.from[message], [](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> m</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> msg</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.room_id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(msg.room_id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The DMs the caller sent.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">DirectMessage</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, my_sent_dms, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> me </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[direct_message].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">me</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> dm</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) { </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> dm.sender.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(me); });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// The DMs the caller received.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">SPACETIMEDB_VIEW</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">DirectMessage</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, my_received_dms, Public, ViewContext ctx) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    auto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> me </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.from[direct_message].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">me</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">](</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> auto&</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> dm</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) { </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> dm.recipient.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">eq</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(me); });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>These views return a query instead of rows, so SpacetimeDB evaluates them with its query engine. When a row changes, it works out which rows of each view changed, instead of running the view again. Each view depends on who asks, so SpacetimeDB keeps one per subscribed player, but each one only covers that player&#x27;s rooms and DMs.</p>
<p>When a player joins a room, <code>my_messages</code> adds the room&#x27;s earlier messages, so the player sees the room&#x27;s history. When they leave, <code>my_messages</code> removes them again.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>note</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>A semijoin can only match columns that have their own single-column index. That&#x27;s why <code>room_member</code> and <code>message</code> both index <code>room_id</code>.</p></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="show-the-messages-on-the-client">Show the messages on the client<a href="#show-the-messages-on-the-client" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Show the messages on the client" title="Direct link to Show the messages on the client" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>On the client, subscribe to <code>player</code> and <code>chat_room</code>, and to the four views. <code>chat_room</code> lists every room, so players can pick one to join, and <code>my_rooms</code> tells you which ones they&#x27;re in. The code below shows the room list when the player asks for it, for example when they open a room picker. <code>player</code> gives you the names of the senders.</p>
<p>Views don&#x27;t keep their rows in any order, so sort the messages on the client. Each message&#x27;s <code>id</code> is larger than the ids of the messages sent before it, so sort by <code>id</code>. To show a conversation with another player, merge the DMs you sent to them with the ones you received from them.</p>
<p>Show the open room or conversation again whenever a message is added or removed. Joining a room adds many messages at once, and leaving it removes them.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { Identity } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { DbConnection, tables } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">./module_bindings</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Shows what&#x27;s on screen: a room or a conversation.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">let</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> () </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {};</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> DbConnection.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">builder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withUri</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">ws://localhost:3000</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withDatabaseName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my-game</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onConnect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscriptionBuilder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onApplied</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscribe</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.player,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.chatRoom,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.myRooms,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.myMessages,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.mySentDms,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tables.myReceivedDms,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      ]);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">build</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">conn.db.myMessages.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onInsert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">conn.db.myMessages.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onDelete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">conn.db.mySentDms.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onInsert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">conn.db.myReceivedDms.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">onInsert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> nameOf</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">identity</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.db.player.identity.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(identity)?.name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">??</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Unknown player</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> listRooms</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> joined </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn.db.myRooms.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.db.chatRoom.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> isJoined </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> joined.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">r</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> r.id </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room.id);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">log</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(isJoined </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">room.name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> (joined)`</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> room.name);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> showRoom</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">roomId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> bigint</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">  render</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> () </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> messages </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn.db.myMessages.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">m</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m.roomId </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> roomId)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sort</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">((</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">a</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">b</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (a.id </span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> b.id </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> -</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> m </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> messages) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">log</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">nameOf</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(m.sender)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">m.text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">  render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> showConversation</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">other</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">  render</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> () </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">SCREEN</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">lock</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Screen</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">db</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> show_conversation</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, other</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">SCREEN</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">lock</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Screen</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Conversation</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(other));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    render</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">db</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> create_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">create_room_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error)) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">                eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not create the room: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> join_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> leave_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">leave_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> send_to_room</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, room_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">send_message_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(room_id, text, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error)) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">                eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not send the message: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> send_to_player</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, recipient</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, text</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">reducers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">send_direct_message_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(recipient, text, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error)) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">                eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not send the message: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p><code>onInsert</code> and <code>onDelete</code> run once for each row that changes, so joining a room can show it many times in a row. If rendering is expensive, render at most once per frame instead.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="whats-next">What&#x27;s next?<a href="#whats-next" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to What&#x27;s next?" title="Direct link to What&#x27;s next?" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>You now have chat rooms and DMs that stay live on every client:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Private tables hold the room members, the messages and the DMs.</li>
<li class="">Reducers check who can send what, and set the sender and time of each message themselves.</li>
<li class="">Views built with the query builder give each player the messages of their own rooms, and their own DMs, and update incrementally.</li>
<li class="">Clients sort the messages, and merge the DMs of each conversation.</li>
</ul>
<p>To adapt it to your app:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><strong>Long histories.</strong> <code>my_messages</code> returns every message of every room the player is in, and the DM views every DM they sent or received. To keep that bounded, delete old messages with a <a class="" href="/docs/tables/schedule-tables">scheduled reducer</a>.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Invite-only rooms.</strong> Add a column to <code>chat_room</code> that marks the room as private, and a reducer that lets members add other players. Make <code>join_room</code> reject private rooms. To keep private rooms out of the public list, make <code>chat_room</code> private, and add a view that returns only the public rooms.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Group DMs.</strong> A group DM is an invite-only room that isn&#x27;t listed.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Blocking players.</strong> Add a private table of blocked players that <code>send_direct_message</code> checks before it sends a DM.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Moderation.</strong> Room names are public, and every client downloads them. To check room names and messages for offensive words, use the same approach as <a class="" href="/docs/how-to/social/unique-usernames">Keep usernames unique and block offensive ones</a>.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Spam.</strong> To stop a player from creating many rooms or sending many messages in a row, store when they last did it, and reject requests that come too soon. The usernames guide uses the same kind of cooldown for name requests.</li>
</ul>
<p>To learn more about the features used here, see <a class="" href="/docs/functions/views">Views</a>, <a class="" href="/docs/tables/indexes">Indexes</a> and <a class="" href="/docs/tables/access-permissions">Access Permissions</a>.</p>
<p>Next, you can show who&#x27;s typing with <a class="" href="/docs/how-to/social/typing-indicators">typing indicators and other short-lived signals</a>.</p></div><footer class="theme-doc-footer
